R1只按原 WP 范围取得历史资格，不臆造总数。R2保留229控制／21批／341精确贡献五元组；R3保留23／76，并保留 M09 optional、U08历史未启动和 M02 NO_REQUIRED 的具体范围。R4当前17／76与B5的16／75区分，Summary后继仅覆盖具名残余。第五唯一 activation 只选择已审 `13fddb…` 候选的92 proposed条目及其原精确内容，当前92 CLOSED／OPEN0，51 P2／41 P3；它不是全部旧提案、整文件、整领域或原生宿主通过。第五7 optional、7 no-required、原6 policy、原13 limits各自保留；410 native、568 schema、158 extra及21 shared／18 deps／85 solewriter均分列。262原贡献＝259显式＋3镜像，6行政 transfer与3纠正不混作新的语义贡献。

本轮受影响 path、clause span 和生产—消费接口必须重新回归对应R1–R5资格。旧 candidate01 G03／G09 FAIL、U03旧FAIL与定向修复、两 advisory MISMATCH、SC-U07-001体内LF原错误及纠正、旧时点 OPEN／WAIT均保留。不能因旧字段字样重开第五已闭项，也不能用92 CLOSED取消第六轮的独立必修。读取指针／hash只验证资格可恢复，不等于新行为 PASS。

**本轮分母与当前边界。** 65条规范裁决为56 required（39 P2／17 P3）、8 optional、1 no-required revision。AN007一般浮点递推的 Float recurrence另1 facet，不加入65；7独立政策＝原6＋GR6-POL-001，也不加入规范 optional／required数。14限制＝原L01–L13本轮刷新＋L14执行与工具元数据缺口。别名、聚合父ID、consumer facet和已经撤销的 UI-PARTY-004不得另造 mandatory或重复根因。optional与policy没有自动修改授权，未选择的未来兼容profile不阻塞56项事实修订。

本轮全量有限静态范围为 R432、formal131／28,851行／20,379有限Markdown块、supplements116（111旧稿secondary结构／身份／primary处置＋5独立保留全文），以及main的38,093 blobs用途处置。20,379块包括标题、结构、历史与scope，不是20,379条行为要求；69份结构化文本没有声称逐物理行手工全文。可得可审的有限范围已闭合，不代表全部机器码、任意未选字符串、原生选择、媒体、地图或完整Demo验收。

L01地图／完整工程、L02图形音频、L03原生执行、L04 Game与运行库、L05可读内嵌程序、L06 animmaker、L07 extendtext、L08字体、L09 soundfont、L10序列化、L11动态扩展配置、L12外部状态服务、L13纯元资料及L14元数据的精确材料、范围、剩余未知与恢复条件都按完整实录保留。限制不是源码未读的替代标签；不能把可得可读单元藏进 native opaque 或 inventory_only。反过来，缺宿主不妨碍完成有限静态事实、修订和静态验收，不得因缺宿主提前放弃这些工作。

**唯一正式写者与首波。** 后继整合 root 对所有实际 `deliverables/final-specification-set/`共享文件和公共行政台账拥有唯一写权限。R6-I是本轮接收角色名，后继必须登记实际root任务身份，不能用角色名伪装已存在UUID。作者只写自己批次的隔离candidate镜像与证据目录，审者只写自己的独立审查目录；任何作者、审者或新子代理都不得直接修改正式共享文件、另一个候选、公共finding／状态／acceptance台账或旧报告。root不得并行安排第二个实际正式写者。

14个首波候选批为 R6-U01内核／所有权／产蛋、U02战斗对象／精神香草、U03钓鱼／解释器、U04 AI失败门／评分／定位、U05行动清理／状态／后期目标、U06作者内容／转换工具、U07动画编辑／播放、U08 Debug入口／反馈／日志、U09编译／缓存／注释、U10捕获／Summary／重学、U11储存导航／图鉴、U12背包／BP店、U13设施／Arena／Palace／活动保存、U14本地化／数据默认／指标／空Flags。每个required恰有一个主批；全部14批可基于固定事实独立准备候选。54正式候选路径、24文件冲突对和100节点／110边的依赖图决定共享接收与整合验收；它们没有作者候选准备的跨批阻塞边，不等于整合或关闭不受阻塞。

28个作者／审者入口是每批 `R6-Uxx-A` 与 `R6-Uxx-R`，交接时均为 `NOT_STARTED`。这是计划身份，不是28个实际运行任务、PID或UUID。首波先准备14份候选，逐批完成候选身份绑定后才能做该批独立审查。当前协作工具容量实核为4、root占1；未来CLI实际并行容量未知，要按已验证可用资源调度并保存排队／启动状态，不把“14首波”写成无上限同时运行承诺，也不悄悄删除未启动批次。

作者请求 `gpt-6.1-sol`、reasoning effort `xhigh`；非作者审者请求同模型、effort `ultra`。二者service tier与speed均为 `default`，禁Fast／priority。审者必须与候选作者独立；曾撰写具体规格者不能成为该变更的唯一终裁者。root的写入接收和正式接受也不能替代非作者的实际整合后审查。无法取得要求的模型／effort时明确登记真实障碍，不能静默换模型、降档或开Fast；配置请求未获有效回显时实际model／effort／tier／speed／UUID标为UNVERIFIED，不能把请求、bundled模型目录或CLI exit0当作有效配置证明。

**真实可用本地CLI及查询。** 本机已实读 `codex-cli 0.162.0-alpha.17.2` 的 `codex --help`、`codex exec --help`、`codex debug --help`、`codex debug models --help` 和 `codex agents --help`。`codex debug models --bundled`不刷新目录，本地所带 `gpt-6.1-sol`列明支持xhigh／ultra，priority标为Fast；它只证明本机目录信息，不证明当前账号可用性或某次任务实际模型。启动前在实际执行宿主重验help，保留返回版本，不用记忆推断不存在的参数。

以下是本机已核参数的调用形式，仅供收到启动授权后的root使用。`repo_root`必须设为上述实际项目路径，`task_output`必须设为root已登记、已创建、属于该入口的绝对目录；两个不同入口不可共享该输出目录。root先把完整中文合同与本批完整七组记录写入实际 `prompt.md`，再启动；命令没有在本次交接准备中运行。

```sh
codex exec -C "$repo_root" -s workspace-write -m gpt-6.1-sol \
  -c 'model_reasoning_effort="xhigh"' -c 'service_tier="default"' \
  --json --color never -o "$task_output/last-message.md" - \
  < "$task_output/prompt.md" > "$task_output/stdout.jsonl" 2> "$task_output/stderr.log"
```

审者的同一调用将 `model_reasoning_effort`改为`"ultra"`，并使用自己独立的prompt／输出路径。`-C`指定工作根不是新增正式写授权；prompt里的逐路径写入allowlist仍须严格执行。launcher同时指定真实cwd，并捕获精确argv、PID、实际UTC起止、stdout／stderr、独立退出码、逻辑ID、请求配置与有效回显。不得遗漏cwd后在仓库根创建文件；本轮INC-R6-001曾真实发生这种创建后移入，现路径不存在或tracked clean不能改写为从未越界。不要复用旧launcher原始日志来伪装新任务已启动。

`codex exec`在本机没有`--cloud`；顶层实验性`codex cloud`也不等于该选项。本地 `exec`不冒称云端任务。若后继明确要求云端，只有在实际工具／API、参数、可用环境和真实回执核定后才使用该入口；未取得云端能力时如实登记，而不是发明flag或UUID。本合同提供的已核可执行入口是本地CLI。

查询优先读取该入口的launcher状态、`task-status.json`、实际stdout JSONL、stderr与阅读／差集台账。真实平台UUID从`thread.started`回显取得，PID从launcher取得；未捕获就保留缺失。`codex agents`是本机真实会话浏览入口，没有在help中出现的`agents list`／`exec status`等子命令不得臆造。`codex resume UUID`会继续会话，不能当只读状态查询。Desktop可用的read_thread／wait_threads必须用真实ID；缓存notLoaded／interrupted不能推翻仍推进的CLI日志。`ps -p`只有结合登记的启动时间、命令和PID复用情况才有意义。任务通信成功排队不代表消息已消费，进程exit0不代表合同全读或候选PASS。

不用查认证、token、额度或私密配置来补证据；不得为该任务安装／下载、开启服务、修改安全配置或重置额度。确需已授权范围之外的新能力或材料时，只阻塞依赖它的部分，继续独立可完成的静态任务，并向root登记准确缺口。

**安全执行与证据分期。** 先懂依赖，再决定是否执行。允许的额外验证仅限已全文审读、依赖已知的有限静态解析、常量算术或安全隔离原协调器／定义；在owned目录内使用显式替身和内存临时输入。不得盲跑未知项目或下载脚本。原EXE／DLL／IL／loader或完整游戏只有在材料/宿主身份明确、对象和依赖已懂、绑定副本及安全隔离范围可控且在已授权范围内时才可运行，并与静态证据分期；条件不足登记精确限制。不得触及真实用户存档、生产账户或未授权外部服务／网络；不得以Marshal／eval等方式把未知序列化载荷当安全数据工具。需要宿主／完整地图／媒体的观察另登记材料与恢复条件，未经授权不扩执行范围。

每次验证分别记录SOURCE_STATIC、DERIVED_VECTOR、TOOL_VALIDATION、ISOLATED_REFERENCE_EXECUTION／REFERENCE_OBSERVATION_WITH_SURROGATES、独立完整行为模型与完整host／Demo。人工向量、Fraction断言、Array语言探针、RIFF／SF2／PCM／PE／CIL／COFF解析、字形／字段结构或安全数据解码不自动成为原参考程序、宿主、声音、像素或完整模型执行。资源加载／音频播放／flash／时间等待／Game.save请求分别只证明源侧请求或替身记录；不能补实际媒体存在、调用成功、固定帧完成、可见／可听结果或持久化结果。

如实际执行原定义或回调，不管旧输出把它叫什么，都如实记源执行。每一尝试保存argv、cwd、实际UTC、runtime、输入身份、替身范围、stdout、stderr、独立进程退出和每向量结果。已发生错误不删除：路径错且未加载R、部分原协调器因替身缺属性停止、无效Purify替身、被覆盖的重复运行都与修正后尝试分列。已预期并捕获的向量异常不算进程失败；tee管道exit0不能当Ruby独立exit0；历史未捕获UTC／退出／stderr不能以mtime、更新时点或重跑倒补。

本轮既有指定探针是18次Ruby尝试：12个已知exit0、4个exit1、2个独立Rubyexit未知；其中1次未加载R、17次进入原定义，exit0仍含无效Purifyv2及F重复运行。向量按O22、CA24含9捕获异常、F当前10、UI有效当前7、K早期7分别保留，不合称全局唯一向量；O3成功／2失败、F三脚本实际五次与K元数据缺失另列。R4隔离4／R5审查隔离4与update0是历史执行分母，不转入后继已执行数。本轮完整宿主、Demo、完整独立玩法模型、真实save与生产服务均0；后继新增执行须独立计量，不继承为新运行PASS。

**逐ID的七组有效验收。** 作者、独立审者和整合后审者必须实际核对以下七组；任一关键项仍缺证据就保留对应未闭合状态，不能仅填一个PASS字段。所需完整逐ID正文必须在交接中展开，以下通用标准不替代该正文。

1. `identity_and_verdict`：canonical ID、别名、根因、领域、当前required／optional／no-required分类、级别与主合同载荷一致。保留原提案／反证／撤销过程，不把子facet或父聚合新增为独立根因。作者不擅自重新裁级或把optional加入本轮必修。
2. `bidirectional_evidence`：源具名unit／事实到实际或有条件消费者再到当前主规格，正式clause反向回到固定源身份、实际范围与输入前提。需要的生产、消费、显示、缓存、保存和后继分支具名；新全文与局部join、本人阅读与当前peer receipt区分。hash／索引／读取成功不能自动给语义PASS，未定位显式caller不宣称native／动态不存在。
3. `premises_and_counterexample`：普通对照、最小反例、合法输入域、真实状态前提、时间／随机短路顺序、取消／故障／部分提交以及负证据范围齐全。失败只说明已经到达的前缀，不补全局回滚、后继完成或永久异常。静态反例与实际运行观察分列；源缺陷与未来兼容选择分开。
4. `minimum_revision_contract`：用独立行为语言作最小规则修订，准确修主合同和必要同步路径，保留已经正确的scope、数值、前提、显示门及正常案例。不能理想化源缺陷、抄源表达式、只改traceability或只在附表列名字而漏正文。测试目录、覆盖附表和跨域引用与新正文一致。
5. `acceptance_and_regression`：逐ID有效正常、边界、失败／取消向量按完整输入前提核对，不能用错误替身结果证明源缺陷。实际受影响R1–R5 span、第五92CLOSED的精确资格、共享消费者及独立控制回归均具名；旧PASS不代新验收。缺宿主时完成可定静态验收，同时把未执行层保持未知；不得冒称已运行宿主或完整模型。
6. `independent_adjudication`：非作者针对确定candidate／integrated载荷作新审，记录实际审者身份、请求／有效模型元数据、读取区间、反证尝试、结果、缺口和被审内容hash。审者报告要明确支持、反对或未能裁定的范围；root保存不采纳意见及理由。作者自检、提出者原支持、CLI完成或root接收文件均不能替代独立审查。
7. `dependencies_and_receivers`：每required唯一主批、实际candidate路径、所有共享文件接收者、冲突段、非阻塞领域依赖和整合／关闭阻塞边准确。14批可并行准备不消除24文件冲突对；root必须合并全部必要写入而非最后覆盖胜出。验收依赖须绑定实际已整合版本，不能靠“已委托”“预计完成”消除pending。

**状态与整合顺序。** 当前所有后续入口保留 `UPDATE_NOT_STARTED`。正式授权后，root先登记新的实际update工作根／分支、已有工作保护、唯一写者、逐任务真实输出allowlist和输入快照；作者创建镜像候选，逐次记录精确变更及预期影响。遇共享文件仍只交候选，由root串行接收。不得自行切换／重置他人工作区、移动main或delivery tag、force push、删除分支／tag、归档或释放环境／工作树；原62额外文件与当前8工作树等保护按真实清单保留，不把旧10工作树快照当当前状态。

候选完成后由独立审者核被审候选SHA与完整七组内容，形成`CANDIDATE_PASS`或具名FAIL／未闭。root接收已通过候选进入实际整合文件，记录来源、冲突解决、接收后SHA和精确受影响clause。随后由独立审者对真实整合后全文、共享接合、56项修订及受影响历史回归复验，取得`INTEGRATED_PASS`。任何后续变更都会使其受影响验收失效，必须对新内容重验，不能保留旧PASS覆盖新编辑。

root在全部必要整合验收、回归、载荷身份、同步路径和阻塞依赖齐备后才能写正式acceptance。`CANDIDATE_PASS`、`INTEGRATED_PASS`、正式接受与`CLOSED`是不同事件：候选可PASS而仍未接收，已接收可待共享复验，已接受可待最终发布／关闭回执。只有对应实际版本的全部关闭条件满足才CLOSED；未启动、部分完成、异常、待材料或未审差集如实保留，不能为交付摘要清零。task进程完成不自动关闭finding。

**最终交付与发布核验。** root是唯一公共记录写者，最终报告逐ID给出原裁决、候选／独立审查／整合／接受／关闭的实际身份及结果，分别列56必修、8optional、1no-required、Float1、policy7、limits14、执行分母、未执行层及INC-R6-001。全部实际新正式文件、测试目录、跨域接合、回归回执、候选／反证／状态及未启动入口必须在完整交接与manifest中具名。无源码、框架实现、R修改或未获授权的optional／policy更改；保护检查只能报告检查所覆盖时点和路径。

在获准的实际报告／修订分支按正常push发布，发布成功须以真实远端对象及每一新增载荷完整回读确认：比较原字节／bytes／SHA256／git blob／mode／type／path、关联commit和目录tree，核完整清单与未声明差集。push超时、进程exit0、远端只存在branch或本地remote-tracking ref都不单独等于每载荷回读成功。保留失败及普通retry，禁止force／删除或把本地对象身份预写成远端成功。

manifest不递归自哈希。先冻结有限语义载荷core C，再由独立的有限发布／交接载荷绑定已实际存在的C和其tree；manifest可明确排除自身，整个Git tree由外部实际对象绑定。最终发布P与P内载荷的核验由外部回执或最终响应记录，不要求P内文件预先含自身commit／目录tree，也不为追求自引用闭合无限重写、重commit、重push。只读回读实际内容，最后报告真实C／P／路径／载荷身份和未闭合发布条件，不制造下一轮虚假identity来消除未知。

持续执行至已获授权的全部修订、独立验收与交付目标完成；跨上下文保留原目标、实读范围、保护约束、原始失败与当前未闭合工作。状态提问或新信息按当前任务的steering处理，回答后继续必要工作；只有明确取消／暂停或不兼容的新目标才替换任务。可完成的静态工作不因缺宿主、长上下文或任务规模提前收尾。完成报告必须自足，读者能从逐ID完整合同和实际证据恢复结论，而不是依赖聊天摘要或“全量PASS”口号。

      "premises_and_counterexample": {
        "input_domain": [
          "已设置正常字符串挑战标识，正常开始，存在可恢复原队伍快照。",
          "该挑战类型记录已有当前胜场5、当前交换数2；可由先前已胜挑战留下。",
          "只调用取消，随后可独立调用结束；无需异常、插件、地图事件可达性或真实存档。"
        ],
        "ordinary_control": "开始后直接设胜利决定并结束：原队伍恢复，当前记录5/2保留，挑战身份重置为未选择。",
        "minimal_counterexample": "开始→取消：原队伍恢复且进行中假，但挑战身份仍tower，类型记录仍5/2。随后结束：外层先按残留身份回收决定0的已重置内层，当前记录变0/0、身份清为-1；内层结束无条件写空原队伍字段，玩家队伍变空。",
        "failure_cancel_partial_commit": {
          "cancel_and_partial_commit": "取消不保存、不刷新、不清外层身份/规则/配置/类型记录。后续结束先提交类型记录清零及外层身份清除，然后无条件写空玩家队伍；非进行中门在队伍写入之后，无回滚。未开始结束置空已在W30说明，本提案不重复该根因，只补残留身份使记录回收仍可发生。"
        },

      "minimum_revision_contract": {
        "rule": "将取消的重置限定为逐项清即时标记、开始位置、决定/胜场/交换/轮次、名单、原队伍/报名/额外数据；外层挑战标识/规则/配置/类型记录，以及内层轮次上限仍保留。补取消后结束的当前记录回收组合对照；保留既有决定0回收与空原队伍写入合同，不声称事件必执行该序列。",
        "preserve_correct_scope": [
          "纯静态设施生命周期范围；原有取消恢复、不保存与W30规则保持。",

        "preconditions": [
          "已设置正常字符串挑战标识，正常开始，存在可恢复原队伍快照。",
          "该挑战类型记录已有当前胜场5、当前交换数2；可由先前已胜挑战留下。",
          "只调用取消，随后可独立调用结束；无需异常、插件、地图事件可达性或真实存档。"
        ],
        "ordinary_control": "开始后直接设胜利决定并结束：原队伍恢复，当前记录5/2保留，挑战身份重置为未选择。",
        "minimal_counterexample": "开始→取消：原队伍恢复且进行中假，但挑战身份仍tower，类型记录仍5/2。随后结束：外层先按残留身份回收决定0的已重置内层，当前记录变0/0、身份清为-1；内层结束无条件写空原队伍字段，玩家队伍变空。",
        "cancel_and_partial_commit": "取消不保存、不刷新、不清外层身份/规则/配置/类型记录。后续结束先提交类型记录清零及外层身份清除，然后无条件写空玩家队伍；非进行中门在队伍写入之后，无回滚。未开始结束置空已在W30说明，本提案不重复该根因，只补残留身份使记录回收仍可发生。",
        "negative_evidence": {
          "source_search_scope": "全部Data/Scripts检索currentChallenge、pbCancel、pbEnd；当前身份写点仅初始化、开始、外层结束；内层reset未写该外层字段。",
          "formal_search_scope": "本轮全文WP55与完整QC/WS/PA/RC目录；查取消、重置与记录回收；未见取消保留外层身份/类型记录的限定或取消后结束的计数对照。"
        },

        "minimal_revision": "将取消的重置限定为逐项清即时标记、开始位置、决定/胜场/交换/轮次、名单、原队伍/报名/额外数据；外层挑战标识/规则/配置/类型记录，以及内层轮次上限仍保留。补取消后结束的当前记录回收组合对照；保留既有决定0回收与空原队伍写入合同，不声称事件必执行该序列。",

          "state_observation_and_later_result": {
            "preconditions": "Arena single battle; attack-phase skill slots are 0; opponent uses Protect before a living user attempts Tackle; normal execution and PP gates pass; Protect is effective, the move can be protected, Unseen Fist is absent; no secondary callbacks, recoil, replacement, or terminal outcome.",
            "trace": "The target protection check marks protection and rejects that target. The hit processor returns false because no target is affected. The surrounding target-processing branch still records the completed-processing state (2), sums only unaffected=false targets and therefore obtains total effectiveness 0, then settles the living user. State 2 ignores the protection flag and assigns skill -2.",
            "reference_expected": "Attacker current skill slot -2, accumulated at round end.",
            "formal_reader_expected": "Reading “真正完成且成功” plus “被保护失败→不写” as a live behavioral rule gives unchanged slot 0. This conflates the labeled state with actual hit success."
          },

      "premises_and_counterexample": {
        "input_domain": "Arena single battle; attack-phase skill slots are 0; opponent uses Protect before a living user attempts Tackle; normal execution and PP gates pass; Protect is effective, the move can be protected, Unseen Fist is absent; no secondary callbacks, recoil, replacement, or terminal outcome.",
        "ordinary_control": "A living user executes an ordinary nonimmune, unprotected single-target move successfully: the completed processing state receives normal effectiveness and assigns skill +1. The state-to-skill table itself is correct when its actual state inputs are supplied.",
        "minimal_counterexample": {
          "preconditions": "Arena single battle; attack-phase skill slots are 0; opponent uses Protect before a living user attempts Tackle; normal execution and PP gates pass; Protect is effective, the move can be protected, Unseen Fist is absent; no secondary callbacks, recoil, replacement, or terminal outcome.",
          "trace": "The target protection check marks protection and rejects that target. The hit processor returns false because no target is affected. The surrounding target-processing branch still records the completed-processing state (2), sums only unaffected=false targets and therefore obtains total effectiveness 0, then settles the living user. State 2 ignores the protection flag and assigns skill -2.",
          "reference_expected": "Attacker current skill slot -2, accumulated at round end.",
          "formal_reader_expected": "Reading “真正完成且成功” plus “被保护失败→不写” as a live behavioral rule gives unchanged slot 0. This conflates the labeled state with actual hit success."
        },
        "failure_cancel_partial_commit": {
          "cancellation_and_partial_failure": "Preserve the existing last-action whole-move failure/no-EOR-backfill rule; distinguish pre-state failures, pending state settled by a later action, and actual target blockage. No rollback guarantee is introduced."
        },
        "impact": "完成目标处理状态不等同实际命中成功，Protect和无目标反射生产者与存活结算门须接合；直接状态评分表/最后行动限定保持。C AF001并入本根因。",
        "evidence_levels_and_execution": {
          "verification": "Static semantic derivation; no reference execution or runtime test."
        }
      },
      "minimum_revision_contract": {
        "rule": "Describe state 1 as written only after execution/PP gates and usage bookkeeping reach its producer. Describe state 2 as completion of the target-processing branch, including zero affected targets, rather than requiring actual success. Qualify settlement to currently nonfainted battlers. Keep the direct state-to-skill table, explicitly restricting protected preservation to state 1; add a live Protect counterexample.",
        "preserve_correct_scope": "仅按原记录与独立裁决的具体前提；不扩展为运行等价或永久缺陷兼容。",
        "root_scope_override": "完成目标处理状态不等同实际命中成功，Protect和无目标反射生产者与存活结算门须接合；直接状态评分表/最后行动限定保持。C AF001并入本根因。",

      "original_full_proposal": {
        "id": "GR6-F-004",
        "title": "Arena skill producer states are described as actual successful execution, and settlement lacks its surviving-battler gate",
        "category": "behavioral mismatch / omitted preconditions",
        "suggested_severity": "high",
        "formal_locations": [
          {
            "path": "deliverables/final-specification-set/combat-requirements/wp56-palace-and-arena-variants.md",

            "end_line": 121
          }
        ],
        "source_locations": [
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/008_Other battle types/004_BattleArenaBattle.rb",
            "start_line": 12,

          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/002_Battler/007_Battler_UseMove.rb",

          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/002_Battler/007_Battler_UseMove.rb",

          },
          {

            "start_line": 369,
            "end_line": 438
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/002_Battler/007_Battler_UseMove.rb",
            "start_line": 501,
            "end_line": 516
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/002_Battler/007_Battler_UseMove.rb",
            "start_line": 590,
            "end_line": 616
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/002_Battler/009_Battler_UseMoveSuccessChecks.rb",
            "start_line": 356,
            "end_line": 364
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/001_Battle/001_Battle.rb",
            "start_line": 448,
            "end_line": 450
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/001_Battle/010_Battle_AttackPhase.rb",
            "start_line": 176,
            "end_line": 182
          }
        ],
        "ordinary_control": "A living user executes an ordinary nonimmune, unprotected single-target move successfully: the completed processing state receives normal effectiveness and assigns skill +1. The state-to-skill table itself is correct when its actual state inputs are supplied.",
        "minimal_counterexample": {
          "preconditions": "Arena single battle; attack-phase skill slots are 0; opponent uses Protect before a living user attempts Tackle; normal execution and PP gates pass; Protect is effective, the move can be protected, Unseen Fist is absent; no secondary callbacks, recoil, replacement, or terminal outcome.",
          "trace": "The target protection check marks protection and rejects that target. The hit processor returns false because no target is affected. The surrounding target-processing branch still records the completed-processing state (2), sums only unaffected=false targets and therefore obtains total effectiveness 0, then settles the living user. State 2 ignores the protection flag and assigns skill -2.",
          "reference_expected": "Attacker current skill slot -2, accumulated at round end.",
          "formal_reader_expected": "Reading “真正完成且成功” plus “被保护失败→不写” as a live behavioral rule gives unchanged slot 0. This conflates the labeled state with actual hit success."
        },
        "additional_precondition_gap": "Settlement traverses existing, nonfainted battlers. A battler already at 0 HP is excluded even if its state is pending; the source does not settle every member of the entire battler array. Early execution/PP failure returns before the assumed-failure state is written. A later surviving battler action may settle an earlier pending whole-move failure; the last-action qualification in P26 is significant.",
        "negative_search_scope": [
          "skill_producers_consumers.txt",
          "Full focal Arena 1–366 and selected UseMove 1–662 / success-check consumer ranges; no Arena-only guard around the ordinary settlement."
        ],
        "cancellation_and_partial_failure": "Preserve the existing last-action whole-move failure/no-EOR-backfill rule; distinguish pre-state failures, pending state settled by a later action, and actual target blockage. No rollback guarantee is introduced.",
        "minimal_fix": "Describe state 1 as written only after execution/PP gates and usage bookkeeping reach its producer. Describe state 2 as completion of the target-processing branch, including zero affected targets, rather than requiring actual success. Qualify settlement to currently nonfainted battlers. Keep the direct state-to-skill table, explicitly restricting protected preservation to state 1; add a live Protect counterexample.",
        "proposed_clean_room_wording": "处理进入目标阶段并走到该阶段末尾时会写入完成状态，即使全部目标被保护或未命中；有目标时，相性只合计未被判无效的目标，全部排除则合计为0。完成状态按相性赋值技槽，合计0赋−2；保护标记只在仍处于失败状态时使技槽保持。结算只处理当前存在且尚未倒下的战斗者。前置资格或PP失败尚未到写入失败状态的时点。",
        "dependencies_and_sync_files": [
          "deliverables/final-specification-set/combat-requirements/wp56-palace-and-arena-variants.md",
          "deliverables/final-specification-set/test-catalog/combat-requirements-wp54-55-56-58.md",
          "audit/source-traceability.md (batch 13)",
          "planning/feature-matrix.md (WP56 row)",
          "WP40/WP43 execution and protection cross-reference; revise only if duplicated language is found"
        ],
        "tests": [
          "Living Tackle against successful Protect: slot 0→-2; damage remains 0.",
          "Normal unprotected nonimmune hit: slot 0→+1.",
          "Direct state1/protected=true fixture preserves prior skill, without presenting it as ordinary Protect behavior.",
          "Settlement excludes an already fainted battler; last-action early whole-move failure retains prior skill, whereas a later eligible settlement may consume its pending state."
        ],
        "status": "PROPOSAL_COUNTERCHECK_COMPLETE_AWAITING_ROOT_I_CLASSIFICATION",
        "verification": "Static semantic derivation; no reference execution or runtime test.",

          "state_observation_and_later_result": {
            "preconditions": "Recorded Palace; living Mega-qualified battler, owner has the required ring, side unused, no external Mega registration, no Encore, at least one ordinarily selectable move. Player chooses Fight; equivalent AI case chooses no switch or item.",
            "trace": "The player returns from the Palace automatic fight path before entering the regular menu containing the Mega toggle. The AI similarly returns from the Palace automatic path before its Mega registration step. Command-phase reset cleared pending registrations before selection.",
            "reference_expected": "This ordinary Palace command path neither exposes the toggle nor registers Mega, despite eligibility.",
            "formal_reader_expected": "The unqualified statement that the original recorded battle’s Mega toggle/registration/execution run normally and RC-W18’s eligibility-only prerequisite can be applied to Palace as well."
          },

      "premises_and_counterexample": {
        "input_domain": "Recorded Palace; living Mega-qualified battler, owner has the required ring, side unused, no external Mega registration, no Encore, at least one ordinarily selectable move. Player chooses Fight; equivalent AI case chooses no switch or item.",
        "ordinary_control": "Tower/Arena player reaches the regular fight menu and may toggle a qualified Mega registration; ordinary AI reaches its Mega registration step after deciding against switching/items. Recording adds no hook that cancels those paths, and omits the registration from the saved data.",
        "minimal_counterexample": {
          "preconditions": "Recorded Palace; living Mega-qualified battler, owner has the required ring, side unused, no external Mega registration, no Encore, at least one ordinarily selectable move. Player chooses Fight; equivalent AI case chooses no switch or item.",
          "trace": "The player returns from the Palace automatic fight path before entering the regular menu containing the Mega toggle. The AI similarly returns from the Palace automatic path before its Mega registration step. Command-phase reset cleared pending registrations before selection.",
          "reference_expected": "This ordinary Palace command path neither exposes the toggle nor registers Mega, despite eligibility.",
          "formal_reader_expected": "The unqualified statement that the original recorded battle’s Mega toggle/registration/execution run normally and RC-W18’s eligibility-only prerequisite can be applied to Palace as well."
        },
        "failure_cancel_partial_commit": {
          "cancellation_and_partial_failure": "A Tower/Arena canceled selection unregisters Mega through ordinary cancellation; Palace’s early automatic return is a separate route. Externally registered Mega and nonstandard overrides remain outside this minimal counterexample."
        },
        "impact": "接受当前原提案的行为差异/具名遗漏及非提出者回源反证；修订范围限定原记录的有效前提、最小规则、正确scope与静态验收，不授运行或永久缺陷兼容。",
        "evidence_levels_and_execution": {
          "verification": "Static semantic derivation; no reference execution or runtime test."
        }
      },
      "minimum_revision_contract": {
        "rule": "Qualify the original-recording Mega statement and RC-W18 to the ordinary menu/AI registration path (Tower/Arena), and add Palace’s bypass as a mode-specific limitation. Retain the record omission and replay inability for actually registered Mega.",
        "preserve_correct_scope": "仅按原记录与独立裁决的具体前提；不扩展为运行等价或永久缺陷兼容。",

          "state_observation_and_later_result": {
            "preconditions": "Arena third round; both sides still have legal healthy reserves; living user successfully uses a recharge-producing attack on that round, survives and wins the coming judgment, with no external replacement or effect rewrite.",
            "trace": "The successful move sets recharge to 2. Ordinary round-end countdown reduces it to 1. Arena invokes full move cancellation, which does not reset recharge and retains current-move identity while recharge is nonzero. The opponent is replaced; the winning user remains. Next round’s execution gate still requires recharge.",
            "reference_expected": "Recharge 1 survives the judgment; the remaining user must recharge next round.",
            "formal_reader_expected": "“取消双方所有多回合招” can be implemented as clearing recharge and allowing a new action next round."
          },

      "premises_and_counterexample": {
        "input_domain": "Arena third round; both sides still have legal healthy reserves; living user successfully uses a recharge-producing attack on that round, survives and wins the coming judgment, with no external replacement or effect rewrite.",
        "ordinary_control": "At the judgment threshold, two-turn charging and the listed ongoing repeat/locked move effects are canceled. The full-cancel option suppresses final-turn Outrage confusion.",
        "minimal_counterexample": {
          "preconditions": "Arena third round; both sides still have legal healthy reserves; living user successfully uses a recharge-producing attack on that round, survives and wins the coming judgment, with no external replacement or effect rewrite.",
          "trace": "The successful move sets recharge to 2. Ordinary round-end countdown reduces it to 1. Arena invokes full move cancellation, which does not reset recharge and retains current-move identity while recharge is nonzero. The opponent is replaced; the winning user remains. Next round’s execution gate still requires recharge.",
          "reference_expected": "Recharge 1 survives the judgment; the remaining user must recharge next round.",
          "formal_reader_expected": "“取消双方所有多回合招” can be implemented as clearing recharge and allowing a new action next round."
        },
        "failure_cancel_partial_commit": {
          "cancellation_and_partial_failure": "Limit the retained state to a judgment survivor. A loser faints and initialization clears its battle effects. Do not use a fixture where ordinary battle already ends before the Arena judgment."
        },
        "impact": "接受当前原提案的行为差异/具名遗漏及非提出者回源反证；修订范围限定原记录的有效前提、最小规则、正确scope与静态验收，不授运行或永久缺陷兼容。",
        "evidence_levels_and_execution": {
          "verification": "Static semantic derivation; no reference execution or runtime test."
        }
      },

        "input_domain": "Palace AI living user h24, no status bonus, old just-switched=false; no Perish Song1 and no no-move/turn>5 branch; exactly one legal nonactive reserve with HP1/total205, no trapping, normal registration succeeds; random0.       "original_full_proposal": {
        "id": "GR6-F-008",
        "title": "Palace best switch candidate needs positive integer HP percentage; zero-percent live candidates use the fallback branch",
        "category": "edge-case precondition omitted",
        "suggested_severity": "medium",
        "formal_locations": [
          {
            "path": "deliverables/final-specification-set/combat-requirements/wp56-palace-and-arena-variants.md",
            "start_line": 78,
            "end_line": 80
          },
          {
            "path": "deliverables/final-specification-set/test-catalog/combat-requirements-wp54-55-56-58.md",
            "start_line": 130,
            "end_line": 131
          }
        ],
        "source_locations": [
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/008_Other battle types/003_BattlePalaceBattle.rb",
            "start_line": 195,
            "end_line": 210
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/008_Other battle types/003_BattlePalaceBattle.rb",
            "start_line": 229,
            "end_line": 243
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/001_Battle/005_Battle_ActionSwitching.rb",
            "start_line": 9,
            "end_line": 33
          },
          {
            "path": "reference/pokemon-essentials/Data/Scripts/011_Battle/001_Battle/005_Battle_ActionSwitching.rb",
            "start_line": 77,
            "end_line": 105
          }
        ],
        "ordinary_control": "A legal reserve with integer HP percentage at least1 becomes the best candidate when it exceeds the current maximum; probability hit registers that candidate and returns before updating the just-switched marker.",
        "minimal_counterexample": {
          "preconditions": "Palace AI living user h24, no status bonus, old just-switched=false; no Perish Song1 and no no-move/turn>5 branch; exactly one legal nonactive reserve with HP1/total205, no trapping, normal registration succeeds; random0. One realizable shape is a Pikachu user at24/100 and a distinct Lapras reserve at1/205.",
          "trace": "Reserve integer percentage is0. The best-candidate selection starts at maximum0 and accepts only a strict increase, so no best index is assigned. Factor30 and random0 produce should-switch=true; the later branch writes just-switched=true and registers the first legal reserve.",
          "reference_expected": "Switch succeeds, just-switched becomes true.",
          "formal_reader_expected": "Taking “取其最高占比m” and “存在最佳候选（m对应成员）” as selecting the mathematical maximum gives that live reserve as best, enters early return, and keeps marker false."
        },
        "negative_search_scope": [
          "Full Palace195–243; only the strict-positive selection path can create the best index; fallback independently checks switch legality.",
          "No inference that zero percentage means fainted: replacement qualification tests actual fainted status/HP."
        ],
        "cancellation_and_partial_failure": "Retain distinction between probability hit, marker update, registration, and successful return. With no legal candidate the later marker may still become true but returnfalse (existing P12).",
        "minimal_fix": "State that best-candidate tracking chooses the first strict maximum above initial0; a legal live reserve can round to0 and leave no best candidate, then probability hits use the first-legal fallback and update the marker. Add an adjacent1-percent control.",
        "proposed_clean_room_wording": "最佳候选只在其整数HP百分比严格高于从0开始的当前最大值时登记；并列保持先遇者。合法存活后备的百分比可整除为0，此时仍没有最佳候选：概率命中后进入后段、写「刚换过」为真，再登记首个合法后备。",
        "dependencies_and_sync_files": [
          "deliverables/final-specification-set/combat-requirements/wp56-palace-and-arena-variants.md",
          "deliverables/final-specification-set/test-catalog/combat-requirements-wp54-55-56-58.md",
          "audit/source-traceability.md (batch 13)",
          "planning/feature-matrix.md (WP56 row)"
        ],
        "tests": [
          "h24, sole reserve1/205, r0, prior markerfalse: factor30, switch true, markertrue.",
          "Same inputs except reserve3/205: factor30, switch true, markerfalse via early return.",
          "No legal reserve, r0: markertrue but switchfalse; keep P12."
        ],

            "initial_state": "Valid active Safari session in its starting reception map or a Safari map, 30 balls, positive steps, decision zero; normal initialized persistent values and available pause menu.",
            "actions": [
              "Open pause menu.",
              "Choose Quit Game (the ordinary game exit, distinct from mode-specific Quit).",
              "Accept quit.",
              "Accept save and any overwrite confirmation; successful normal save."
            ],

      "premises_and_counterexample": {
        "input_domain": [
          "Ordinary map/pause UI reachable, player and normal registered save values valid; no plugin/replacement handlers.",
          "Safari session active and current map satisfies its region query. For the contest counterpart, valid chosen member and configuration, active session with ample unexpired time.",
          "No unrelated callback/state change during the confirmation sequence; user accepts Quit Game and Save, and any required overwrite confirmation.",
          "Normal serializer/File IO succeeds. No runtime execution or actual save file was used in this review."
        ],
        "ordinary_control": {
          "without_mode": "Dedicated Save is available when system saving is enabled, and normal save can serialize state.",
          "active_mode": "Dedicated Save is hidden, but Quit Game remains visible and opens the same save screen. Activity state is not ended or cleared by this effect before saving."
        },
        "minimal_counterexample": {
          "initial_state": "Valid active Safari session in its starting reception map or a Safari map, 30 balls, positive steps, decision zero; normal initialized persistent values and available pause menu.",
          "actions": [
            "Open pause menu.",
            "Choose Quit Game (the ordinary game exit, distinct from mode-specific Quit).",
            "Accept quit.",
            "Accept save and any overwrite confirmation; successful normal save."
          ],
          "expected_from_reference": "Save serializer receives global metadata containing the still-active Safari session, then Quit Game closes the menu and sets scene to nil. No Safari end or return-to-start consumer ran in this path.",
          "conflicting_formal_expectation": "WP53 line 244 permits normal save persistence only for inactive/unstarted/ended sessions."
        },
        "failure_cancel_partial_commit": {
